## Panda Financial Academy learned (~18:55 CDT)
- User asked Foil to learn https://pandafinancial.org/learn/. Panda’s Automated Vault runs on Base: a USDC zap converts roughly half to WETH, opens a per-wallet Aerodrome Slipstream WETH/USDC concentrated-liquidity position, stakes it for AERO emissions, and pays the user 80% of rewards in USDC; Panda keeps 20% (19% treasury, 1% keeper). Minimum USDC deposit is $5; no deposit/withdrawal fee or lock-up.
- Keeper checks about every 35 seconds, waits for a 6-hour sustained out-of-range condition before moving, chooses roughly ±1%–8% width, caps moves at 3/day with ≥2 hours between, and may wait up to 24 hours in a crash. Only the depositor wallet can withdraw; emergency exit returns WETH/USDC as held.
- Important constraints/risks from Panda’s own Academy: Automated Vault has not had an independent security audit; users bear ETH exposure, divergence/impermanent loss, out-of-range downtime, AERO price, keeper-key/slippage, and smart-contract risk. Panda is explicitly unavailable to U.S. persons or people located in the U.S. Its separate DeFi Tools area is only a UI for 0x, Steakhouse, Morpho, and Jumper; Panda cannot reverse those third-party actions.

## Panda Financial deep dive (user asked to learn it all, 2026-09-29 ~19:05 CDT)
- pandafinancial.org: tiny (~July 2026) DeFi product on Base. Live product = Automated Vault V2: deposits USDC (Zap, min $5) or WETH+USDC (Split, min $2.50 each) into one Aerodrome WETH/USDC Slipstream position per deposit; Rust keeper bot keeps it in range (±1–8%, home ±2%) to harvest AERO emissions (not swap fees).
- Fees: 20% of AERO rewards (19% treasury, 1% keeper), compile-time constants confirmed on BaseScan. Keeper rules public: 6h out-of-range confirmation, 2h cooldown, max 3 moves/day, crash valve.
- Traction 2026-09-29: $2,494.63 TVL, 18 depositors. Vault contract 0xf5D48b04D4529D652BFAE172E8Fb47b389Db920e (verified, Solidity 0.8.34), 261 txs, real deposits/claims/rebalances. Keeper wallet matches whitepaper; one deposit from xyopanda.base.eth (team).
- DeFi Tools = thin wrappers over 0x swaps (0.30% total), Jumper bridge (0.40%), Steakhouse/Morpho USDC vault (0.05% deposit), Morpho WETH/USDC lending (0.05% deposit). Arcade and Grove "coming soon". NO token yet — they warn about impersonator scams.
- Security: NO independent audit (stated in whitepaper); single-EOA owner/keeper keys, no multisig/timelock; pooled-AERO dust (≤0.01 rolls to next claim); fully dependent on Aerodrome emissions. No incidents found.
- Team: essentially anonymous. Only named person: XyoPanda (@XyoPanda) — "Contributor @_PandaFinancial, Founder Nakamura Labs". Official X @_PandaFinancial joined July 2026, 202 followers. No legal entity, no team page.
- LOOPERS CONNECTION UNVERIFIED: user said "is a fellow looper" but no public evidence found anywhere. Offered to dig into XyoPanda's X for the link.
- Restrictions: not offered to US/UK persons or sanctioned jurisdictions, 18+, VPN circumvention prohibited, non-custodial, as-is.
- Reception: no reviews/complaints found. Name-confusion risk: unrelated Austrian forex broker panda-financial.com (WikiFX "No Regulation") and 2023 "Panda Financ3" presale token — do NOT attribute those to pandafinancial.org.
- No published APR/track record; only live per-position estimates (gross, before 20% fee, in-range only). Research notes: ~/workspace/research_notes/panda-financial-deep-dive-20260929-2356/

## FALSE ALARM correction: Foil RH wallet "drain" (~23:20 CDT)
- Foilbox run #71 reported Foil's RH wallet dropped 0.0010626 → 0.0002667 ETH (~75%) with "cause unresolved," verified via "two independent RPCs."
- Re-verified directly: balance is 0.0010637 ETH on rpc.mainnet.chain.robinhood.com, nonce 433 — matches the 22:52 CDT baseline (0.0010626) minus claim gas. NO DRAIN OCCURRED.
- The 0.0002667 read was bogus: robinhood-rpc.publicnode.com closed the connection on re-check (no response), so the worker's "second RPC" likely returned a stale/failed read that got counted as verification. Historical eth_getBalance also fails on mainnet RPC (no archive state) — don't treat either as ground truth without cross-checking nonce + a fresh read.
- Lesson: a balance anomaly with NO corresponding nonce increment is a bad read, not a drain. Check nonce first.
